login: Inject MetricsService into LoginDisplayHostCommon

Inject metrics::MetricsService* into ash::LoginDisplayHostCommon and
its subclasses (LoginDisplayHostMojo and LoginDisplayHostWebUI),
replacing g_browser_process->metrics_service() when constructing
OobeMetricsHelper in LoginDisplayHostCommon (following
https://crrev.com/c/8623018) and WizardController in
LoginDisplayHostMojo::EnsureOobeDialogLoaded(),
LoginDisplayHostWebUI::StartWizard(), and
LoginDisplayHostWebUI::OnStartAppLaunch(). This removes the last
g_browser_process usage from login_display_host_common.cc.

Pass metrics::MetricsService* from ShowLoginWizardFinish(),
ShowLoginWizard(), and UserAddingScreenImpl::Start(), and thread it
from ChromeBrowserMainPartsAsh::PostProfileInit() through
ChromeSessionManager::Initialize(), StartAutoLaunchKioskSession(), and
StartKioskSession() (matching https://crrev.com/c/7611191).

metrics::MetricsService is created in
ChromeBrowserMainParts::SetupMetrics() during PreCreateThreads() and
destroyed in BrowserProcessImpl::StartTearDown() after the main RunLoop
exits, whereas LoginDisplayHostCommon holds a ScopedKeepAlive and
schedules its destruction before the main RunLoop terminates, so
metrics_service outlives LoginDisplayHostCommon.

Dependency injection flow:
  ChromeBrowserMainPartsAsh::PostProfileInit
  +--> ChromeSessionManager::Initialize
       +--> StartAutoLaunchKioskSession
            +--> StartKioskSession
                 +--> LoginDisplayHostWebUI

  ShowLoginWizard / ShowLoginWizardFinish
  +--> LoginDisplayHostWebUI / LoginDisplayHostMojo

  UserAddingScreenImpl::Start
  +--> LoginDisplayHostMojo

  LoginDisplayHostWebUI / LoginDisplayHostMojo
  +--> WizardController (metrics_service)
  +--> LoginDisplayHostCommon
       +--> OobeMetricsHelper (metrics_service)

TAG=agy

Append non-SVCB fallback endpoints in DnsTaskResultsManager

When a host resolution finishes and stores results in HostCache,
HostCache::Entry::ConvertToServiceEndpoints() appends a non-SVCB
fallback ServiceEndpoint (with empty ConnectionEndpointMetadata) at the
end of the endpoint list. However,
DnsTaskResultsManager::UpdateEndpoints() omitted this fallback endpoint
whenever HTTPS record metadata was present for a domain, so intermediate
results of ServiceEndpointRequest were inconsistent with the final
result.

This delays QUIC connection attempts in QuicSessionPool::AsyncDnsJob.
When the QUIC version is known from Alt-Svc and SVCB is optional, a QUIC
attempt can use either an endpoint whose HTTPS ALPNs include "h3" or the
non-SVCB fallback endpoint. If the HTTPS record doesn't advertise "h3"
(e.g. "HTTPS 1 ." without alpn, or alpn="h2") while Alt-Svc does,
intermediate updates (e.g. AAAA and HTTPS completed while A is still in
flight) had no endpoint usable for QUIC, and the attempt had to wait
until all DNS queries finished, even though the final result allows it
through the fallback endpoint. A scan of popular sites found this
configuration on some high-traffic origins.

Update DnsTaskResultsManager::UpdateEndpoints() to append the non-SVCB
fallback ServiceEndpoint using non-hint IPv4/IPv6 addresses whenever
real addresses are available, matching ConvertToServiceEndpoints(). Hint
addresses are excluded since they come from SVCB/HTTPS records.

TCP-based connection attempts are not expected to change in practice:
Chrome adds the default "http/1.1" ALPN to HTTPS metadata and ignores
HTTPS records when none of them allows the default ALPN, so an endpoint
with metadata is already usable for TCP.

[Blink] Fix layout lifecycle crash on gesture long-press

Fix a layout lifecycle assertion crash in `HitTestResult::GetPosition()`
during touch long-press gestures.

When a touch long-press occurs, `HandleGestureLongPress()` calls
`SelectClosestWordFromHitTestResult()` to select the word. If word
selection fails or is cancelled by a `selectstart` event listener, it
falls back to placing a caret via `SetCaretAtHitTestResult()`. Both
methods query `HitTestResult::GetPosition()`, which asserts (DCHECK)
that the document lifecycle is at least `kPrePaintClean`.

However, gesture selection previously lacked proper layout lifecycle
updates:
* `HandleGestureLongPress()` only invoked `UpdateStyleAndLayoutTree()`,
  skipping layout geometry and pre-paint property tree updates needed
  by `SelectClosestWordFromHitTestResult()`.
* When `SelectClosestWordFromHitTestResult()` dispatches `selectstart`,
  author JavaScript can mutate styles or DOM and cancel selection.
  This re-dirties the document lifecycle mid-gesture before the
  fallback caret placement in `SetCaretAtHitTestResult()`.

While mouse press paths were long ago guarded with full lifecycle
updates in `MouseEventManager::HandleMousePressEvent()`, gesture paths
were missing these guards.

Change details: In `blink::SelectionController`:
* `HandleGestureLongPress()`: Advance all lifecycle phases through
  pre-paint by replacing `UpdateStyleAndLayoutTree()` call with
  `UpdateAllLifecyclePhasesExceptPaint()`.
* `SetCaretAtHitTestResult()`: Add the same call to ensure any
  mid-gesture mutations (and other callers like
  `HandleGestureTwoFingerTap()`) operate on clean layout geometry.
* Add regression unit tests in `selection_controller_test.cc`, covering
  both pre-gesture dirty layout and mid-gesture selectstart mutations.
* Add WPT crashtest in `longpress-selection-crash.html`.

Revert CHECK_EQ to DCHECK_EQ in MTPDeviceDelegateImpl::RunTask

In MTPDeviceDelegateImpl::RunTask, init_state_ CHECK_EQ was recently
converted from DCHECK_EQ. Revert it back to DCHECK_EQ to prevent release
crashes while keeping the DCHECK for debug builds.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/1ca354c0af800000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

Revert "cros: Fast-shutdown renderers and skip window hide animation on exit"

This reverts commit 6ba4e77d8b5f1cb1072f5d06c0792739c85a4d5c.

Reason for revert: Causes MSan use-of-uninitialized-value / UAF
crashes during ProfileDestroyer::Timeout() teardown in browser_tests
(FPNM/NonIOWarningBrowserTest.MultiFileOKShowsDialog/* and
HoldingSpaceClientImplTest.AddItemOfType) on Linux ChromiumOS MSan

Failure Link:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/Linux%20ChromiumOS%20MSan%20Tests/61796/overview
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/Linux%20ChromiumOS%20MSan%20Tests/61798/overview

Original change's description:
> cros: Fast-shutdown renderers and skip window hide animation on exit
>
> On ChromeOS, ExitIgnoreUnloadHandlers() handles SIGTERM from
> session_manager and passes ShutdownType::kBrowserExit when all
> browsers are closeable or ShutdownType::kEndSession otherwise. Add
> browser_shutdown::ShouldFastShutdownRenderers() returning true on
> ChromeOS for ShouldIgnoreUnloadHandlers() and kBrowserExit, and pass
> it as skip_unload_handlers, ignore_workers, ignore_keep_alive, and
> ignore_pending_reuse to RenderProcessHost::FastShutdownIfPossible() in
> OnShutdownStarting() and TabStripModel::CloseWebContentses().
>
> With ignore_workers and ignore_keep_alive enabled on ChromeOS,
> renderer processes running service workers or pending fetch(keepalive)
> requests are terminated immediately when shutdown starts to stay
> within the 3 s session_manager budget; session restore state lives in
> the browser-side NavigationController and is unaffected. Also disable
> visibility-change animations on the browser widget on ChromeOS in
> BrowserView::Close() once shutdown has started so
> wm::WindowAnimations does not clone the window layer tree during
> process exit.
>

3bee1554498faefd3c3f3a92798a5f097d5c5f41
parent
43a50329a51e44f846e8693e6484bbc2b96bb9e3 [ diff ] 
[html-in-canvas] Fix filter dirty bit clearing

This patch moves clearing of `filter_on_effect_node_dirty_` and
`backdrop_filter_on_effect_node_dirty_` out of `PaintLayer`'s filter
builder methods (which are now `const`) and into
`PaintLayer::ClearFilterAndBackdropFilterOnEffectNodeDirty()`, called
from `PaintPropertyTreeBuilder::UpdateForSelf` once the object's last
fragment updates.

Previously, `UpdateCompositorFilterOperationsForFilter` and
`UpdateCompositorFilterOperationsForBackdropFilter` cleared the shared
`PaintLayer` dirty bits immediately. An earlier fragment in a multicol
container would clear the dirty bit before later fragments updated,
causing those fragments to early-return and keep stale filter
operations after moving into a `<canvas content=drawable>` subtree.

Fixed: 574759287

tree
da9ef60167aaa62187180276b457e65ca1f590fe
parent
67e2963ad7ac3bfb102aeb3b50b7080274dfce70 [ diff ] 
[BottomBar] Resume the education tour after the bar is hidden

Opening the tab switcher during the tooltip tour ends the tour.
BottomBarMediator dismisses the education flow whenever the bar is
hidden, and BottomBarEducationCoordinator treats a tooltip that closes
because its anchor was hidden as a user dismissal, so every remaining
step is skipped.

Add BottomBarEducationCoordinator#onBottomBarHidden() and call it from
BottomBarMediator when the bar is hidden, for example by the tab
switcher or omnibox focus. It still dismisses the bubble, but pauses a
running tour and resumes the pending step once the bar is visible
again. A tooltip that the system hides no longer counts as dismissed
or advances the tour.

Also record the tooltip Dismissed metric in dismiss() based on the last
shown step, so a paused or re-anchoring tooltip still gets exactly one
Dismissed when the tour ends.

Before: https://drive.google.com/file/d/1MvooBW70X6py7_6JcLHClI5v7G9MQLIl/view

After: https://drive.google.com/file/d/1yqXgSlfLKmIcf5WNvsntWIr073XXCm0i/view

[CacheStorage] Remove obsolete legacy cache_dir migration

tree
9917de74c66703091670cc7aa2af6274615844ff
parent
7884a0ffb844f411f89d3a62d9bfd026ed85d221 [ diff ] 
[CacheStorage] Remove obsolete legacy cache_dir migration

`proto::CacheStorageIndex::Cache::cache_dir` was added in Chrome M48
(crrev.com/1414033002, October 2015), and its migration unit test was
removed in Chrome M56 (crrev.com/2407733004, October 2016).

Remove the obsolete `!cache.has_cache_dir()` migration branch in
`SimpleCacheLoader::ReadAndMigrateIndexInPool()` that moved legacy
SHA-1-named directories via `base::Move()`, and instead return an empty
`proto::CacheStorageIndex` so `CacheStorage` starts fresh when an index
entry is missing `cache_dir`. Also remove the now-unused `HexedHash()`
helper and dangling `MigratedLegacyCacheDirectoryNameTest` friend
declaration.

401ac9081cc91517e43bcd22491a40f134d8743a
parent
8be291e22d21a2d70de1fe153a92e54a7e027235 [ diff ] 
Don't open media bubble over empty mic/camera state

ContentSettingMediaStreamBubbleModel requires a non-empty
microphone/camera state in PageSpecificContentSettings; its constructor
enforces this with CHECK(CameraAccessed() || MicrophoneAccessed()). The
state can be cleared asynchronously while the omnibox icon or activity
indicator chip remains visible and clickable. For instance, Safety Hub's
unused site permission auto-revocation stops active capture, clearing
state while the indicator lingers through its hide delay. Clicking the
indicator in that window constructs the bubble over an empty state and
triggers the CHECK.

Guard the production construction path in
ContentSettingMediaImageModel::CreateBubbleModelImpl by returning
nullptr when the live state is empty. Update the three bubble-opening
surfaces (ContentSettingImageView, PermissionDashboardController, and
WebUIContentSettingImageControl) to treat a null model as a stale
indicator by dismissing or refreshing it instead of opening a bubble.

Bug: 1516745, b/548343050, b/524520967

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
Don't treat a 1xx response followed by a socket close as a success.
📋 Metadata
commit
a156403fc8c3c666346427ad01ab8e6af6e9dd41
[ log ]
author
Matt Menke <mmenke@chromium.org>

Mon Oct 26 02:00:58 2026
tree
8640a693ee45e107e5af382e99c6705a1e28bb2e
parent
8add1345fdf90a931068608055236a2a8dafd430 [ diff ] 
Don't treat a 1xx response followed by a socket close as a success.

This can theoretically lead to confusion, since we return the headers as
if it were a successful 2xx response (we do return the 1xx response
code), but it's safest to just fail the request, since we didn't receive
the actual response.

The Fetch spec is not very clear on what is expected in this case, since
it just has a loop for reading 1xx results, with no exit-on-error case,
but this seems likely more compliant with the spec than treating the 1xx
as the final response code, with a made-up empty response body.

This CL does not make us retry in this case, unlike the no-data case on
a stale socket.

3d463313f1ec6c6c9e62739cafd9eeeda1b58e98
parent
717e637346a1114585c1efd16929d053ae4f8967 [ diff ] 
[WebNN] Don't fold a reshape into a constant with a pending permutation

ConstantFoldingTransformer folds a transpose of a constant by recording
a pending permutation on the new constant, which the service applies to
the data later. Folding a following reshape replaced the constant with
one built from the new shape only, dropping the pending permutation, so
the service used the original data layout as if it were already
transposed and produced wrong results.

A reshape of transposed data generally cannot be expressed as a single
permutation, so keep the reshape operator when its input constant has a
pending permutation.

2fd054a1ed2dcab5630b848a989d0f7b80d2336b [ diff ] 
[contextual-search] Handle tab close and navigation in session handle

When a browser tab attached to a Contextual Search / Contextual Tasks
session is closed or navigates to a non-equivalent URL, the session's
TabContextState previously retained the tab until the next turn was
submitted.

This CL adds session-level support for reactive tab close and
navigation handling:
- Add ContextualSearchSessionHandle::OnTabClosedOrNavigated() so tab
  close or cross-URL navigation immediately removes the tab from
  TabContextState::attached and restored (and notifies
  SubscribeTabContext() subscribers), deletes non-submitted FileInfo
  entries via DeleteFile(), and retains submitted tabs in
  displaced_submitted_tabs_ so the next turn's TakeRemovedContexts()
  reports their LensOverlayRequestId in removed_contexts and marks
  their FileInfo superseded.
- Update TakeRemovedContexts() to report non-superseded displaced
  submitted tabs when kLensDeleteContextOnPageNavigation is enabled,
  preserving newly staged tokens when a navigated tab is re-attached.

[ContextualTasks] Avoid UAF in OnBackButtonExpandsSidePanel
📋 Metadata

fbb38c2a8b977a137a130cf6eeea63fc290d02a3
[ log ]
author

tree
bfc1632e6f5854110e4bd11afe1fa10d7f422d8d
parent
bb393bb6202217054b8ae5fac774a4c505205538 [ diff ] 
[ContextualTasks] Avoid UAF in OnBackButtonExpandsSidePanel

OnBackButtonExpandsSidePanel() cached the side panel WebContents in a
raw pointer and then called MoveTaskUiToNewTab(), which can
synchronously destroy that WebContents (e.g. when the panel is not
showing an AI page, the page is opened in a new tab and the panel is
closed, freeing the cached WebContents). The stale pointer was then
used to restore navigation history.

Hold a WeakPtr to the side panel WebContents across
MoveTaskUiToNewTab() and skip the history restoration if it was
destroyed. Also bail out if the original tab went away.

Add a unit test that destroys the side panel WebContents during
MoveTaskUiToNewTab(); it reports heap-use-after-free under ASan
without this fix.

[IOS] Allow configured API keys in ComposeboxQueryControllerIOS

On iOS developer builds, `use_official_google_api_keys` is false and the
API key is provided via `google_api_key` or `Info.plist`, causing
`google_apis::IsGoogleChromeAPIKeyUsed()` to return false and failing
Composebox attachment uploads.
Override `IsGoogleChromeApiKeyUsed()` on iOS to also check
`google_apis::HasAPIKeyConfigured()`.

Skip ShardBrowserTest.BrowserInitiatedAboutBlank without BFCache

The test assumes the browser-initiated about:blank navigation swaps
BrowsingInstances, which only happens with proactive BrowsingInstance
swaps. Those are disabled when BackForwardCache is disabled, so the
test fails on linux-bfcache-rel. Skip it in that configuration until
the expectations are fixed.

🌺 Revert "[Android] Match InProcessBrowserTest feature list"

This reverts commit 231082cd7aaa69d1def498bbefc14f34cdd4e654.
Culprit: 231082cd7aaa - "[Android] Match InProcessBrowserTest feature list" (refs/heads/main@{#1726572}, https://crrev.com/c/8626336)

Reason for revert: consistently fails android-desktop-17-arm64-rel-hylia-tests (and 4 other Android CI builders).

Mechanism: Modifying AndroidBrowserTest::AndroidBrowserTest() to disable
features::kPrewarm, features::kPreconnectToSearch,
performance_manager::features::kTransientKeepAlivePolicy, and
features::kRestartNetworkServiceUnsandboxedForFailedLaunch across all
android_browsertests caused deterministic failures starting at
refs/heads/main@{#1726572} across 5 CI builders
(android-desktop-17-arm64-rel-hylia-tests #1512 with 23 failures,
android-desktop-17-x64-rel-brya-tests #2327 with 12 failures,
android-desktop-x64-rel-15-tests #23937 with 2 failures,
android-desktop-15-x64-fyi-rel #11045, and android-15-tablet-x64-fyi-rel
#22367). Disabling background prewarming/preconnect changes
ChromeBrowserTestsActivity startup and compositor/IME view attachment
timing (InputMethodManager: Ignoring showSoftInput() as view is not
served / ImeTracker: onFailed at PHASE_CLIENT_VIEW_SERVED), causing WebUI
focus tests (CrElementsFocusTest.*, FlagsUiBrowserTest.App,
NewTabPageFocusTest.AppFocus, CrExtensionsFocusTest.*),
TabBottomSheetBrowserTest.*, and SecurePaymentConfirmationBrowserTest.*
to fail focus assertions or time out in RunLoop::Run().

android_browsertests::FlagsUiBrowserTest.App (and 22 sibling tests in
android_browsertests) has failed in 5/5 recent builds across Android CI
builders, every attempt in every one of them. This is not flake: it
reproduces on every run, so it blocks the CQ for every unrelated CL that
touches this suite. The regression appears at refs/heads/main@{#1726572}.

Example failing run:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chrome/builders/ci/android-desktop-17-arm64-rel-hylia-tests/1512

To run/deflake this test on CQ before relanding, add the following
footer to your CL description:
  Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chrome.android.desktop:android-desktop-17-arm64-rel-hylia-tests,chromium.android.desktop:android-desktop-x64-rel-15-tests|android_browsertests

Reverting to green the tree first. Please re-land once the failure is
understood and covered; this revert is about restoring CQ throughput,
not about the merit of the change.

Original change's description:
> [Android] Match InProcessBrowserTest feature list
>
> Previously, AndroidBrowserTest left default search engine preconnect
> (kPreconnectToSearch, which fires with a 0 ms startup delay on Android),
> search engine prewarm (kPrewarm, which warms up a background prerender
> renderer and compositor during startup),
> kRestartNetworkServiceUnsandboxedForFailedLaunch,
> kTransientKeepAlivePolicy, and in-product help features enabled, unlike
> desktop's InProcessBrowserTest.
>
> Disable these background features by default and block IPH via
> ScopedIphFeatureList in AndroidBrowserTest to match
> InProcessBrowserTest, avoiding unnecessary startup overhead and
> nondeterministic background work. Dedicated tests for these features can
> still enable them via ScopedFeatureList or ScopedIphFeatureList.
>
> Measured impact: On a 5-test local android_browsertests batch (5 runs on
> emulator-5554), batch wall time drops from 17.24 s to 16.66 s (-3.4%, or
> -118 ms/test) in default run-from-apk mode and from 15.41 s to 14.86 s
> (-3.5%, or -108 ms/test) after package compilation, with mean GTest
> runtime dropping by 4.4% to 5.2%.
>
> Bug: 576702252
> Test: run_android_browsertests --gtest_filter=AndroidBrowserTest.*
> Change-Id: I4175d0a74d484dbee615e19e191f9eb8b7a89707
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8626336
> Reviewed-by: Calder Kitagawa <ckitagawa@chromium.org>
> Commit-Queue: James Cook <jamescook@chromium.org>
> Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1726572}

43d3e60dc0734a0d5d15dd41ccc18c93549242ad
parent
d4d613355ad137685e4f35deed6a3697cd9d5202 [ diff ] 
SVG text: keep combining marks with their base character

x/y/dx/dy/rotate lists were consumed and applied per code point, so a
combining mark took a list value and was shaped and positioned apart
from its base. A code point that extends the previous typographic
character is now the "middle" of that character: it still consumes a
value of every list, which keeps the lists indexed by addressable
characters, but its x and y are dropped and its dx and dy are
accumulated into the next typographic character.

Bug: 41007184

tree
e2c0a9bfb6b557b6d1e513a53884074d01f003d1
parent
3e761aa8075cf306f28f630f3c2c8e7ef7764a26 [ diff ] 
Fix paragraph boundaries in text with ::first-letter

When an element has ::first-letter, its text node's layout object is a
LayoutTextFragment whose TransformedText() starts at TextStartOffset()
rather than at the start of the node. StartOfParagraphAlgorithm() and
EndOfParagraphAlgorithm() used the DOM offset of the position directly
as an index into that text, so a purely cosmetic ::first-letter style
moved paragraph boundaries in white-space-preserving text. For example,
EndOfParagraph() just before the '\n' in "1ab\nde" returned 6 instead of
3, unlike the identical text without ::first-letter.

Convert between DOM offsets and fragment indices in both functions, fix
the expectation in endOfParagraphFirstLetterPre that recorded the wrong
result, and add StartOfParagraph tests with and without ::first-letter.

[WebAudio] Skip resampling when AudioBus sample_rate is 0.0f.

The comment on AudioBus::CreateBusFromInMemoryAudioFile states that
sample_rate == 0.0f decodes the audio file at its native sample rate.
However, the implementation only returned early when sample_rate
matched the file sample rate. Otherwise, it passed 0.0f to
TryCreateBySampleRateConverting, which did not validate new_sample_rate,
divided by zero (sample_rate_ratio = +inf), and returned a 0-frame
AudioBus with sample_rate = 0.0f.

When sample_rate is 0.0f, use the decoded bus sample rate so callers
keep the file's native sample rate. Also validate new_sample_rate in
TryCreateBySampleRateConverting so invalid or negative target sample
rates return nullptr.

Also spanify WebAudioBus::ChannelData() to return base::span<float>
directly from AudioChannel::MutableSpan(), removing raw pointer indexing
and UNSAFE_TODO wrappers in AudioDecoder and tests.

Verification:
- Unit tests: out/Default/blink_platform_unittests --gtest_filter="AudioBusTest.*:AudioArrayTest.*:WebAudioBusTest.*" -> 10/10 passed

TAG=agy
CONV=ad0dae3b-e580-49e3-813f-03ec72fd2bb6

ea7df9eb606f55425f0b703e80f7027d886a5415 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Fix and re-enable SaveCardInfobarEGTest tests

Re-enable 10 disabled tests in save_card_infobar_egtest.mm.

Several issues prevented these tests from passing:
1. In crrev.com/c/8065879, fillAndSubmitFormWithID: was modified to
   call setFormFillMaxStrikes:numStrikes for card CreditCardSave__5454,
   defaulting numStrikes to 0. In multi-step test flows (such as
   triggerCreditCardSaveModal and testUMA_Upstream_UserDeclines*),
   dismissing the initial bottom sheet records a strike, but the second
   form submission then reset the strike count back to 0. With 0
   strikes, Chrome showed the bottom sheet again rather than falling
   back to the infobar banner. We now default numStrikes to std::nullopt
   and only call setFormFillMaxStrikes when numStrikes.has_value().
2. Strike delay enforcement (kAutofillUpstreamEnforceStrikeDelay)
   required a multi-day delay between strikes, blocking sequential
   strikes during test execution. Disabled this feature for test cases.
3. The field trial testing config enables
   kAutofillSaveCardBottomSheetStrikeLimitIos, which raises the
   number of times the bottom sheet is shown before falling back to the
   infobar from 1 to 3. Disable this feature so tests verify the default
   flow.
4. testSaveCardBottomSheetShowsLoadingAndConfirmationAfterAcceptPushed
   hung in EarlGrey synchronization while UIActivityIndicatorView spun
   infinitely during loading. Wrapped that section in a
   ScopedSynchronizationDisabler.

testInfobarWithoutBadge remains FLAKY_ as its page loading flakiness is
tracked separately under crbug.com/440644620.

Tests: Re-enabled 10 SaveCardInfobarEGTest EarlGrey tests:
- testUMA_Upstream_UserDeclinesBottomSheetAndInfobar
- testUMA_Upstream_UserDeclinesBottomSheetAcceptsInfobar
- testSaveCardBottomSheetShowsLoadingAndConfirmationAfterAcceptPushed
- testNotOfferLocalSave_MaxStrikesReached
- testStickySavePromptJourney
- testOfferLocalSave_WithInfobar
- testUpstreamCVCValidation_WithShortInvalidInput
- testUpstreamCVCValidation_WithLongInvalidInput
- testUpstreamCVCValidation_WithNonDigitInput
- testUpstreamCVCValidation_WithValidInput

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

[iOS] Enable BubblePresenterTestCase pull to refresh tests on iPad

This CL enables four pull-to-refresh tests in BubblePresenterTestCase on
iPad:
- testPullToRefreshIPHAfterReloadFromOmniboxAndDisappearsAfterNavigation
- testPullToRefreshIPHShouldDisappearOnEnteringTabGrid
- testPullToRefreshIPHShouldNotShowOnPageLoadFail
- testPullToRefreshPerformAction

These tests were previously disabled in crrev.com/c/8630043 due to
flakiness from split screen. The underlying issue is that split screen
simulation using `openNewWindow` is unreliable on iPad simulators for
test environments. This CL avoids `openNewWindow` and instead mocks the
trait collection by setting `horizontalSizeClass` to
`UIUserInterfaceSizeClassCompact` on the key window, which achieves the
same UI layout without flakiness, and asserts that `OpenSplitScreen()`
is only invoked on iPad.

Additionally, this CL fixes a focus issue in `ReloadFromOmnibox()` where
the omnibox text input would occasionally resign first responder
immediately after being focused on iPad. It now ensures the element
receives and keeps focus before sending the Return key event.

Finally, this CL fixes the erroneous test documentation comment above
`testPullToRefreshIPHShouldDisappearOnEnteringTabGrid`, which was a
copy-paste from `testPullToRefreshIPHShouldNotShowOnPageLoadFail`.

This CL was produced during AI week as part of
go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome, an agent-driven effort to
re-enable disabled and flaky tests.

521441860f5056571081713ee25da002549bc63a [ diff ] 
[iOS] Re-enable CommercePushNotificationClientTest tests

This CL fixes and re-enables TestNotificationInteraction and
TestBrowserInitialization in CommercePushNotificationClientTest.

Previously, both tests registered their OCMExpect for
[application_handler_ openURLInNewTab:] at the end of the test, after
HandleNotificationInteraction or OnSceneActiveForegroundBrowserReady
had already synchronously dispatched openURLInNewTab:. When
EXPECT_OCMOCK_VERIFY was added to TearDown, verification failed because
the call happened before the expectation was set. Setting OCMExpect
before triggering the action resolves the issue.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

3a0358040a10526cea912a434a459d33eb4c5743

3b17e1f2dd12312df0f6a97e4c1c737ec10ce508 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Fix PageContextWrapperTest.PopulatePageContextWithShadowDom on iOS 27

Wait-free innerText extraction in getInnerTextIncludingShadowDom()
appends open shadow root text chunks after the host node's light-DOM
innerText using '\n' as the separator. However, node.innerText was not
trimmed before joining (unlike direct text node children of a shadow
root, which are trimmed).

On iOS < 27 WebKit, reading document.body.innerText when a <p> element
is followed by shadow host <div> elements without rendered light-DOM
text emitted two trailing newlines ("light-dom text\n\n"), producing
three newlines when joined with the first shadow text chunk. On iOS 27
WebKit, trailing newlines after <p> are no longer emitted when
subsequent siblings produce no light-DOM text, causing
PopulatePageContextWithShadowDom to fail.

Trim node.innerText in getInnerTextIncludingShadowDom() so light-DOM
and shadow-DOM text chunks are consistently separated by a single
newline across iOS versions, and re-enable
PageContextWrapperTest.PopulatePageContextWithShadowDom.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

[BottomBar] Add UMA metrics for the education bubble, dialog and tour

There are no metrics for how users respond to bottom bar education, so
its experiment arms can't be compared.

Record the outcome of the default and Gemini bubbles and the opt-in
dialog in Android.BottomBar.Education.Onboarding.{OnboardingType}.Event.

Record Shown and Dismissed for each tour tooltip in the existing
Android.BottomBar.IPH.{IPHType}.Event histograms, with a new
TabSwitcher variant, instead of adding a parallel set of tooltip
histograms. The standalone bottom bar IPHs don't show while education
is enabled, so a user is counted by one source or the other, not both.

04697603846de3ef80f2fcfb494ff49aead661a8 [ diff ] 
[BottomBar] Defer the NTP theme tip once bottom bar education has shown

The NTP theme tip bottom sheet can open on top of bottom bar education;
for example, a new tab opened from the tour's "+" step shows the sheet
over the tour. The tip isn't an IPH, so Feature Engagement doesn't hold
it back.

Keep an in-memory flag in BottomBarEducationCoordinator, set when the
bubble, the tooltip tour or the opt-in dialog shows, and skip the theme
tip in NewTabPageCoordinator while it's set. The flag lives as long as
the process, so it survives the activity recreate from "Try it now" and
resets when the process restarts. The tip is only marked as shown when
it actually shows, so it's deferred, not dropped.

Bug: 574590271

73df37596bf71a17827e5380cf3b5df5f79abb01 [ diff ] 
cros: Fast-shutdown renderers and skip window hide animation on exit

On ChromeOS, ExitIgnoreUnloadHandlers() handles SIGTERM from
session_manager and passes ShutdownType::kBrowserExit when all
browsers are closeable or ShutdownType::kEndSession otherwise. Add
browser_shutdown::ShouldFastShutdownRenderers() returning true on
ChromeOS for ShouldIgnoreUnloadHandlers() and kBrowserExit, and pass
it as skip_unload_handlers, ignore_workers, ignore_keep_alive, and
ignore_pending_reuse to RenderProcessHost::FastShutdownIfPossible() in
OnShutdownStarting() and TabStripModel::CloseWebContentses().

With ignore_workers and ignore_keep_alive enabled on ChromeOS,
renderer processes running service workers or pending fetch(keepalive)
requests are terminated immediately when shutdown starts to stay
within the 3 s session_manager budget; session restore state lives in
the browser-side NavigationController and is unaffected. Also disable
visibility-change animations on the browser widget on ChromeOS in
BrowserView::Close() once shutdown has started so
wm::WindowAnimations does not clone the window layer tree during
process exit.

TAG=agy
CONV=acea79f2-f3ae-48fd-a985-33bcb9d543e4

Bug: 575822306, 572927279

[Smart Card] Add SmartCardContextRelease feature

Add a runtime-enabled feature, and a matching chrome://flags entry, to
gate the upcoming SmartCardContext.release() method. It lets a page
release a PC/SC context and its connections deterministically, instead
of waiting for the context to be garbage collected.

Chromestatus: https://chromestatus.com/feature/5141169477648384

[Smart Card] Add ENABLE_SMART_CARD buildflag

Replace the raw ENABLE_SMART_CARD define with a buildflag in
//third_party/blink/public:buildflags, and migrate its consumers.

The raw define was only set for //content/browser, so it never reached
isolated_web_app_entitlements_browsertest.cc, where the smart card
entitlement checks were silently compiled out.

42441cebaacef06a8b9bd17cd500c88bff2299f2
parent
edd107109066a4cf754f4b2b6005bbe0c5714341 [ diff ] 
[webnn] Document why npu_concatenated_driver_version is not merged in operator|=

`npu_concatenated_driver_version` is a per-EP reporting format
statement that is queried directly by IsNpuDriverVersionBlocked()
per individual device, rather than read from the merged session-level
workarounds.

This CL documents on the field declaration why it is intentionally
omitted from the merge operation, preventing future confusion.

tree
a82fe33ee6adcbeb317f060672aeb68131161330
parent
56ebe9d2d4c8033e0d5cab7347f2d4816159ce8a [ diff ] 
Metrics: Drop native pointer in UmaSessionStats

UmaSessionStats::GetInstance() is a process-lifetime base::NoDestructor
singleton already accessed directly by most JNI entry points in
uma_session_stats.cc. Storing its address in sNativeUmaSessionStats as a
raw long in Java to dispatch umaResumeSession and umaEndSession is
unnecessary.

Route umaResumeSession and umaEndSession through static JNI functions
that call UmaSessionStats::GetInstance() directly, and remove
sNativeUmaSessionStats and init().

TAG=agy
CONV=d7896fff-bb31-4ee0-9555-287077259ab8

Bug: 40794873

SiteIsolation: Add shards for default SiteInstances

Allows an embedder to split sites that don't need a dedicated process
into "shards". Sites in different shards never share a renderer
process, while sites within a shard can still share one. This is
intended for Android WebView, which doesn't use full Site Isolation, so
that a set of sites can be kept out of the renderer used for all other
web content without giving each site its own process (known as WebView
Site Sharding).

Each BrowsingInstance has one default SiteInstance per shard. They all
use the same default site URL, and are distinguished by the shard on
their SiteInfo (SiteInfo::shard()). Shards are only supported with
default SiteInstances, not yet default SiteInstanceGroups (which WebView
doesn't use), and this is CHECKed.

ProcessLock records the shard of each process that is not locked to a
site. The shard is assigned when an allows-any-site process is first
used, after that it can't change so ProcessLocks for different shards
never compare equal. RenderProcessHostImpl::IsSuitableHost() and
ChildProcessSecurityPolicy compare the process's ProcessLock shard
against the destination SiteInfo's shard. Shards only divide up sites
that don't need a dedicated process.

//content:
- SiteShard (content/browser/site_shard.h) is an enum with two values:
  kDefault, for all sites that aren't specified by the embedder, and
  kEmbedderSites.
- BrowserContext::SetSitesForEmbedderShard(sites) puts a set of sites
  (net::SchemefulSite) in the embedder shard. It can only be called
  once, before any RenderProcessHost in the BrowserContext is used, and
  CHECKs that sites are HTTP(S) and that default SiteInstanceGroups,
  single-process mode and the Rust ChildProcessSecurityPolicy
  ProcessState aren't in use. This could be extended to multiple shards
  if needed.
- SiteInfo::shard() is computed in SiteInfo::Create() from the origin
  that will be committed (so data: URLs use their initiator's shard and
  LoadDataWithBaseURL uses the base URL's) and is part of SiteInfo
  equality and ProcessLock comparisons. This is skipped when the
  BrowserContext has no shards.
- BrowsingInstance keeps one default SiteInstance per shard.
- ProcessLock::shard() is assigned when an allows-any-site process is
  first used (ChildProcessSecurityPolicyImpl::SetProcessIsUsed(), via
  ProcessLock::SetIsUsed()); changing it is a CHECK failure.
  RenderProcessHostImpl::IsSuitableHost() won't reuse a process across
  shards, and the Citadel checks ensure processes only host content
  from their own shard.
- When shards are configured, error pages for blocked renderer-initiated
  requests commit in the destination's process rather than the current
  one (NavigationRequest::ComputeErrorPageProcess()), so a process never
  ends up hosting content from another shard.

//android_webview:
- AwBrowserContext::SetSitesForEmbedderShard() puts the given sites in
  the embedder shard and CHECKs it isn't in single-process mode. Nothing
  calls it yet; the API plumbing will follow separately.

Bug: 576113096
